Result: 训练于人工不确定性上的探针在识别真实不确定性方面优于未使用人工不确定性训练的探针，在困难数据上校准性显著提升，同时在简单数据上性能损失极小。

Conclusion: 人工不确定性注入是一种有效且实用的替代方案，可以在缺乏真正困难训练数据的情况下，提升语言模型不确定性量化方法的校准性能。

Abstract: In safety-critical applications, language models should be able to characterize their uncertainty with meaningful probabilities. Many uncertainty quantification approaches require supervised data; however, finding suitable unseen challenging data is increasingly difficult for large language models trained on vast amounts of scraped data. If the model is consistently (and correctly) confident in its predictions, the uncertainty quantification method may consistently overestimate confidence on new and unfamiliar data. Finding data which exhibits enough uncertainty to train supervised uncertainty quantification methods for high-performance models may therefore be challenging, and will increase in difficulty as LLMs saturate datasets. To address this issue, we first introduce the problem of inducing artificial uncertainty in language models, then investigate methods of inducing artificial uncertainty on trivially easy data in the absence of challenging data at training time. We use probes trained to recognize artificial uncertainty on the original model, and find that these probes trained on artificial uncertainty outperform probes trained without artificial uncertainty in recognizing real uncertainty, achieving notably higher calibration on hard data with minimal loss of performance on easy data.

</details>


### [145] [Creativity Bias: How Machine Evaluation Struggles with Creativity in Literary Translations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13596)
*Kyo Gerrits,Rik van Noord,Ana Guerberof Arenas*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了自动评估指标和LLM-as-a-judge在文学翻译中的表现，发现它们与专业评估者的相关性较差，LLM存在偏向机器翻译、惩罚创造性译文的系统性偏见，尤其在诗歌等文学体裁中表现更差，表明当前自动评估工具存在根本性局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估现有自动评估工具（AEMs和LLM-as-a-judge）在文学翻译中与专业人工评估的一致性，判断它们能否替代耗时的手动标注工作。

Method: 构建了一个多维度文学翻译数据集，涵盖三种翻译模态（人工翻译、机器翻译、译后编辑）、三种体裁和三种语言对，由经验丰富的专业文学翻译家对创造力（创造性转换与错误）进行详细标注，并比较AEMs和LLM-as-a-judge的评估结果。

Result: AEMs和LLM-as-a-judge与专业评估在创造力评估上相关性较低；LLM-as-a-judge存在系统性偏差，偏好机器翻译文本，惩罚创造性和文化适应性强的译文；在诗歌等文学性更强的体裁中表现更差。

Conclusion: 当前自动评估工具在文学翻译评估中存在根本性局限，需要开发新的工具以正确识别而非惩罚非常规的创造性翻译。

Abstract: This article investigates the performance of automatic evaluation metrics (AEMs) and LLM-as-a-judge evaluation on literary translation across multiple languages, genres, and translation modalities. The aim is to assess how well these tools align with professionals when evaluating translation, creativity (creative shifts & errors), and see if they can substitute laborious manual annotations. A dataset of literary translations across three modalities (human translation, machine translation, and post-editing), three genres and three language pairs was created and annotated in detail for creativity by experienced professional literary translators. The results show that both AEMs and LLM-as-a-judge evaluations correlate poorly with professional evaluations on creativity, with LLM-as-a-judge showing a systematic bias in favour of machine-translated texts and penalising creative and culturally appropriate solutions. Moreover, performance is consistently worse for more literary genres such as poetry. This highlights fundamental limitations of current automatic evaluation tools for literary translation and the need to create new tools that do not frequently consider out of routine translations as errors.

</details>


### [146] [Edit-level Majority Voting Mitigates Over-Correction in LLM-based Grammatical Error Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13624)
*Takumi Goto,Yusuke Sakai,Taro Watanabe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需训练的推理方法，通过对单个LLM生成的多个候选结果进行编辑级别的多数投票，有效缓解了语法纠错中的过度修正问题，在7种语言的9个基准上优于贪心解码和MBR解码，且对提示指令不敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在语法纠错中经常出现过度修正（over-correction）问题，即模型不仅修正真正的语法错误，还错误地修改了原本正确的表达，导致输出质量下降。

Method: 提出一种训练无关（training-free）的推理方法，对单个LLM生成的多个候选纠错结果进行编辑级别的多数投票（edit-level majority voting），无需修改模型结构或额外训练。

Result: 在英语、捷克语、德语、乌克兰语、韩语、印地语和罗马尼亚语共7种语言的9个基准上，该方法在大多数情况下优于贪心解码（greedy decoding）和最小贝叶斯风险解码（MBR decoding），并且纠错质量对不同指令提示保持稳定。

Conclusion: 所提出的编辑级多数投票方法能有效缓解过度修正问题，是一种简单、稳定且跨语言泛化性强的语法纠错推理策略，并已开源相关工具库。

Abstract: Grammatical error correction using large language models often suffers from the over-correction issue. To mitigate this, we propose a training-free inference method that performs edit-level majority voting over multiple candidates generated by a single model, without requiring model modifications or additional training. Across nine benchmarks covering English, Czech, German, Ukrainian, Korean, Hindi, and Romanian, the proposed method outperforms both greedy and MBR decoding in most cases. Moreover, it yields stable correction quality regardless of the instruction prompts used. We release two repository supporting GEC datasets loading and LLM inference.

</details>


### [147] [Prefix Teach, Suffix Fade: Local Teachability Collapse in Strong-to-Weak On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13643)
*Kaiyuan Liu,Ziyuan Zhuang,Yang Bai,Bing Wang,Rongxiang Weng,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现强到弱在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中，对完整响应序列进行密集监督并非总是有效，并提出“局部可教性崩溃”概念——教师反馈在后续轨迹段可能缺乏局部对比度。基于此，作者设计了一种轨迹特定的释放规则，通过测量教师相对于学生Top-K候选集的边际、按句子段聚合，并在检测到BIC式变化点时截断密集OPD监督。在Qwen3模型族的实验表明，该方法在多个领域内基准和领域外任务上均优于标准全轨迹OPD。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文献认为在OPD中提供教师对完整响应序列的密集反馈应能单调提升学生性能，但作者在强到弱OPD实验中发现这一假设有时不成立，即后续轨迹段虽存在非零的师生优势，却缺乏局部对比度，导致监督效率下降。

Method: 提出局部可教性崩溃的概念，并设计轨迹特定的释放规则：测量教师相对于学生Top-K候选集的边际，按NLTK分词的句子段聚合边际值，在检测到BIC式向下变化点时截断后续密集OPD监督，从而将监督集中在教师反馈仍具区分性的轨迹区域。

Result: 在Qwen3模型族的强到弱蒸馏实验中，该释放规则在五个领域内基准上一致优于标准全轨迹OPD，且相比基线蒸馏方法更好地保持了模型在领域外任务上的能力。

Conclusion: 有效的强到弱OPD不仅需要评估教师指导的可用性，还需评估其局部效用，确保生成的反馈保持可教性；密集反馈应集中在教师边际仍具判别力的区域，而非均匀覆盖整个响应。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student model on its own rollouts using dense feedback from a stronger teacher. Prior literature suggests that, provided teacher feedback is available, supervising the full sequence of response tokens should monotonically improve performance. However, we demonstrate that this assumption sometimes fails to hold in strong-to-weak OPD settings. While later segments of a generated trajectory may still exhibit a non-zero teacher-student advantage, they frequently lack the local contrast that makes dense feedback effective for prioritizing student learning. We term this failure mode local teachability collapse. The resulting principle is straightforward: supervision should concentrate on trajectory regions where the teacher's feedback remains discriminative, rather than uniformly covering the entire response. We operationalize this principle through a trajectory-specific release rule. This rule measures the teacher's margin over the student's top-$K$ candidate set, aggregates this margin across NLTK-tokenized sentence segments, and truncates dense OPD supervision upon detecting a BIC-style downward change point. Experimental results across strong-to-weak distillation tasks using the Qwen3 model family indicate that this release rule consistently outperforms standard full-trajectory OPD across five in-domain benchmarks at various student scales. Furthermore, compared to baseline distillation methods, our approach better preserves model capabilities on out-of-domain task. These results suggest that effective strong-to-weak OPD requires evaluating not only the availability of teacher guidance but also its local utility, ensuring that the generated feedback remains teachable.

</details>


### [148] [Fine-tuning with Hierarchical Prompting for Robust Propaganda Classification Across Annotation Schemas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13663)
*Lukas Stähelin,Veronika Solopova,Max Upravitelev,David Kaplan,Ariana Sahitaj,Premtim Sahitaj,Charlott Jakob,Sebastian Möller,Vera Schmitt*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以意图为导向的宣传技巧新分类法，并通过四种语言模型在多维度上（模型组合、分类法影响、提示策略）评估其在社交媒体宣传检测中的表现。研究发现微调至关重要，分层提示方法（HiPP）在低一致性分类法上效果显著，HQP数据集为未来研究提供了更具挑战性的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交媒体上的宣传检测因文本噪声大、篇幅短以及标注一致性低而面临挑战，现有分类法在标注一致性和意图聚焦方面存在不足。

Method: 提出意图驱动的宣传技巧新分类法，与已有的高一致性分类法对比；构建HQP数据集；沿三个维度（模型组合、分类法影响、提示策略）评估四种语言模型（GPT-4.1-nano、Phi-4 14B、Qwen2.5-14B、Qwen3-14B）；提出层次化提示方法（HiPP），先预测细粒度技巧再聚合。

Result: 微调将弱零样本基线转化为有竞争力的系统，并揭示了基模型隐藏的方法差异；Qwen系列模型在跨分类法中表现最强，Phi-4 14B持续优于GPT-4.1-nano；HiPP方法在微调后及低一致性分类法上优势明显，在高一致性分类法上也保持竞争力。

Conclusion: HQP数据集为宣传的战略目标提供了更丰富的视角，并成为未来鲁棒、真实场景检测研究的一个具有挑战性的基准。

Abstract: Propaganda detection in social media is challenging due to noisy, short texts and low annotation agreements. We introduce a new intent-focused taxonomy of propaganda techniques and compare it against an established, higher-agreement schema. Along three dimensions (model portfolio, schema effects, and prompting strategy) we evaluate the taxonomies as a classification task with the help of four language models (GPT-4.1-nano, Phi-4 14B, Qwen2.5-14B, Qwen3-14B). Our results show that fine-tuning is essential, since it transforms weak zero-shot baselines into competitive systems and reveals methodological differences that are hidden using base models. Across schemas, the Qwen models achieve the strongest overall performance, and Phi-4 14B consistently outperforms GPT-4.1-nano. Our hierarchical prompting method (HiPP), which predicts fine-grained techniques before aggregating them, is especially beneficial after fine-tuning and on the more ambiguous, low-agreement taxonomy, while remaining competitive on the simpler schema. The HQP dataset, annotated with the new intent-based labels, provides a richer lens on propaganda's strategic goals and a challenging benchmark for future work on robust, real-world detection.

</details>


### [149] [RTLC -- Research, Teach-to-Learn, Critique: A three-stage prompting paradigm inspired by the Feynman Learning Technique that lifts LLM-as-judge accuracy on JudgeBench with no fine-tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13695)
*Andrea Morandi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出RTLC方法，通过三阶段提示策略（研究→教学→批评）将黑盒LLM转化为集成思维裁判，在JudgeBench基准上显著提升成对判断准确率（Claude 3.7 Sonnet从64.6%提升至78.6%），超越自洽性多数投票等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM-as-a-judge已成为开放式生成任务的标准评估工具，但在JudgeBench等基准上，即使强指令微调模型在客观正确性成对项目上的表现仅略优于随机水平，亟需提升LLM裁判的判断准确性。

Method: 提出RTLC三阶段提示配方：Stage 1将输入包装在固定教学支架中，移植费曼学习技术（学习→教学→发现差距→简化）到LLM提示中；Stage 2在温度0.4下抽取N=10个独立候选判断；Stage 3作为自身批评者，交叉比较候选集与原始问题，在温度0下输出一个经批评的判断。

Result: 在JudgeBench-GPT的350个难成对项目上，Claude 3.7 Sonnet的成对准确率从64.6%（单次原始提示）提升至78.6%（RTLC批评10个），绝对提升14.0个百分点。RTLC优于N=10自洽性多数投票（77.7%）和零样本首次候选（74.0%）。三阶段消融实验：Teach-to-Learn支架贡献+9.4pp，N=10边缘化贡献+3.7pp，显式批评贡献+0.9pp。

Conclusion: RTLC在成本-准确率前沿上各工作点均优于自洽性；揭示了JudgeBench四个类别（知识、推理、数学、编码）的错误预算分布；RTLC与事后裁判分数校准正交组合，两种干预在实践中呈乘性复合增益。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge is now the default measurement instrument for open-ended generation, but on the public JudgeBench benchmark even strong instruction-tuned judges barely scrape past random on objective-correctness pairwise items. We introduce RTLC, a three-stage prompting recipe -- Research, Teach-to-Learn, Critique -- that promotes a single black-box LLM into an ensemble-of-thought judge with no fine-tuning, retrieval, or external tools. Stage 1 wraps the input in a fixed pedagogical scaffold porting the Feynman Learning Technique (study $\to$ teach $\to$ find gaps $\to$ simplify) into LLM prompting. Stage 2 draws N=10 independent candidate verdicts at temperature 0.4. Stage 3 acts as its own critic, cross-comparing the candidate set against the original question to emit one critiqued verdict at temperature 0. On JudgeBench-GPT (350 hard pairwise items), Claude 3.7 Sonnet's pairwise accuracy climbs from 64.6% (single-shot vanilla prompt) to 78.6% (RTLC critique-of-10) -- an absolute 14.0-percentage-point gain. RTLC also beats N=10 self-consistency majority voting (77.7%) and a zero-shot first candidate (74.0%). A clean three-step ablation attributes +9.4 pp to the Teach-to-Learn scaffold, +3.7 pp to N=10 marginalisation, and +0.9 pp to explicit critique. We discuss the cost-accuracy frontier (RTLC sits above self-consistency at every working point), the error-budget breakdown across the four JudgeBench categories (knowledge, reasoning, math, coding), and how RTLC composes orthogonally with post-hoc judge-score calibration, with the two interventions compounding multiplicatively in practice.

</details>


### [150] [Children's English Reading Story Generation via Supervised Fine-Tuning of Compact LLMs with Controllable Difficulty and Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13709)
*Qian Shen,Fanghua Cao,Min Yao,Shlok Gilda,Bonnie J. Dorr,Walter L. Leite*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过微调80亿参数的LLM（如Llama 3.1 8B、Mistral 7B、Gemma 2 9B），基于专家设计的儿童阅读课程和GPT-4o/Llama 3.3 70B生成的故事语料，实现了在难度可控性上超越零样本大模型（GPT-4o和Llama 3.3 70B）的儿童英语阅读故事生成，且几乎无安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的儿童故事通常难度过高，且运行成本高昂，阻碍其在教育场景中的广泛部署。

Method: 利用专家设计的儿童阅读课程及其对应的GPT-4o和Llama 3.3 70B生成故事，设计不同微调实验对三个8B参数LLM进行微调，生成新的英语阅读故事并进行定量和定性评估。

Result: 经过适当微调设计的8B LLM生成的儿童英语阅读故事在难度相关指标上优于零样本GPT-4o和Llama 3.3 70B，且几乎无安全风险。

Conclusion: 微调后的小型LLM能够以可控的难度、安全的方式生成符合儿童兴趣的英语阅读故事，适合教师、家长和儿童在课堂及家庭中广泛使用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are widely applied in educational practices, such as for generating children's stories. However, the generated stories are often too difficult for children to read, and the operational cost of LLMs hinders their widespread adoption in educational settings. We used an existing expert-designed children's reading curriculum and its corresponding generated stories from GPT-4o and Llama 3.3 70B to design different experiments for fine-tuning three 8B-parameter LLMs, which then generated new English reading stories that were subjected to quantitative and qualitative evaluation. Our method prioritizes controllability over scale, enabling educators to target reading levels and error patterns with a compact, affordable model. Our evaluation results show that with appropriate fine-tuning designs, children's English reading stories generated by 8B LLMs perform better on difficulty-related metrics than those from zero-shot GPT-4o and Llama 3.3 70B, with almost no discernible safety issues. Such fine-tuned LLMs could be more broadly used by teachers, parents, and children in classrooms and at home to generate engaging English reading stories with children's interests, controllable difficulty and safety.

</details>


### [151] [Dense vs Sparse Pretraining at Tiny Scale: Active-Parameter vs Total-Parameter Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13769)
*Abdalrahman Wael*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 在tiny-scale（sub-25M参数）预训练场景下，MoE（Mixtral风格，4专家top-2路由）在激活参数匹配时验证损失显著优于稠密模型（1.5788 vs 1.6545），但在总参数量匹配时略逊于稠密模型（1.5608 vs 1.5788），且训练过程中激活匹配优势持续扩大而总参差距迅速缩小。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在极小规模预训练场景下，系统比较稠密Transformer与MoE Transformer的性能差异，特别是激活参数匹配与总参数匹配两种对比设置下的真实收益。

Method: 固定LLaMA-style decoder训练流程（tokenizer、数据、优化器、学习率、深度、上下文长度、归一化、评估协议一致），将稠密基线等比例调整宽度以匹配MoE的激活参数或总参数；MoE使用4专家、top-2路由、Switch式负载均衡和router z-loss。

Result: 三种子实验：稠密激活匹配验证损失1.6545，MoE 1.5788（MoE优势0.0758）；稠密总参匹配验证损失1.5608，MoE 1.5788（稠密优势0.0180）。训练中激活匹配优势持续增长，总参匹配稠密优势迅速缩小。

Conclusion: 在sub-25M参数tiny-scale预训练中，MoE在激活参数匹配下显著提升验证损失，但同等总存储容量下未超越稠密模型。

Abstract: We study dense and mixture-of-experts (MoE) transformers in a tiny-scale pretraining regime under a shared LLaMA-style decoder training recipe. The sparse model replaces dense feed-forward blocks with Mixtral-style routed experts. Dense baselines are modestly width-resized to tightly match either active or total parameter budgets, while tokenizer, data, optimizer, schedule, depth, context length, normalization style, and evaluation protocol are held fixed. Our best sparse recipe uses four experts, top-2 routing, Switch-style load balancing, and router z-loss. In a three-seed full-data comparison, the dense active-match model reaches 1.6545 +/- 0.0012 best validation loss, the MoE reaches 1.5788 +/- 0.0020, and the dense total-match model reaches 1.5608 +/- 0.0025. This yields a matched-active gap of 0.0758 +/- 0.0021 in the MoE's favor and a matched-total gap of 0.0180 +/- 0.0020 in the dense model's favor. Across training, the matched-active advantage grows while the matched-total dense advantage narrows sharply. In this sub-25M-parameter regime, MoE therefore improves validation loss under active-parameter matching but does not surpass dense training at equal total stored capacity.

</details>


### [152] [Where Does Reasoning Break? Step-Level Hallucination Detection via Hidden-State Transport Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13772)
*Tyler Alvarez,Ali Baheri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出将大语言模型在多步推理中的幻觉检测问题重新定义为隐藏状态轨迹动力学问题。通过对比PCA（教师模型）和蒸馏BiLSTM（学生模型），在单次前向传播中定位首个错误步骤，超越了传统的全输出置信度评分方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测器多在轨迹级别操作（为完整输出分配单一置信度），无法定位首个错误步骤，且通常需要多次采样。本文旨在将幻觉检测从轨迹级别精细化到步骤级别，实现单次前向传播中首个错误步骤的定位。

Method: 1）构建标签条件教师模型：通过对比PCA（contrastive PCA）为每条推理轨迹构建特定投影，提取7种几何过渡特征对每个步骤评分；2）蒸馏BiLSTM学生模型：从教师模型蒸馏得到，可直接在原始隐藏状态上运行，无需推理时的标签信息；3）理论证明对比PCA是最优投影，且单次定位成立条件为首个错误产生正向传输边际。

Result: 在ProcessBench、PRM800K、HaluEval和TruthfulQA四个基准上，教师和学生模型均优于基于熵、探针和注意力的基线方法。教师模型在不同语言模型和数据集间稳定迁移，但学生模型在分布偏移下性能显著下降，这一差距由蒸馏理论预测。

Conclusion: 将步骤级幻觉检测重新定义为轨迹动力学问题，识别出部署的核心障碍：在分布偏移下保持对比传输边际。学生模型的脆弱性揭示了蒸馏方法的根本局限。

Abstract: Large language models hallucinate during multi-step reasoning, but most existing detectors operate at the trace level: they assign one confidence score to a full output, fail to localize the first error, and often require multiple sampled completions. We frame hallucination instead as a property of the hidden-state trajectory produced during a single forward pass. Correct reasoning moves through a stable manifold of locally coherent transitions; a first error appears as a localized excursion in transport cost away from this manifold. We operationalize this view with a label-conditioned teacher that builds a trace-specific contrastive PCA lens and scores each step with seven geometric transition features, and a deployable BiLSTM student distilled from the teacher that operates on raw hidden states without inference-time labels. We prove that contrastive PCA is the optimal projection for a transport-separation objective between first error and correct states, and that single-pass first error localization holds whenever the first error creates a positive transport margin over preceding correct transitions. On ProcessBench, PRM800K, HaluEval, and TruthfulQA, both models outperform entropy-based, probing-based, and attention-based baselines in-domain; the teacher transfers stably across language models and datasets, while the student collapses under shift, a gap our distillation theory predicts. These results recast step-level hallucination detection as a problem of trajectory dynamics and identify the central obstacle to deployment: preserving the contrastive transport margin under distribution shift.

</details>


### [153] [An LLM-Based System for Argument Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13793)
*Paulo Pirozelli,Victor Hugo Nascimento Rocha,Fabio G. Cozman,Douglas Aldred*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的端到端系统，用于将自然语言文本中的论证重构为抽象论证图。系统采用多阶段流水线，逐步识别论证组件、选择相关元素并揭示其逻辑关系，最终表示为包含前提和结论两种组件类型以及支持、攻击和削弱三种关系类型的有向无环图。通过人工评估和定量基准评估，系统能够有效恢复论证结构并在基准数据集上取得合理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论证是人类推理的核心，但现有方法在自动化地从自然语言文本中提取和重构论证结构方面仍面临挑战。本文旨在设计一种可扩展的、基于LLM的端到端系统来解决这一问题。

Method: 构建多阶段LLM流水线，包含论证组件识别、相关元素选择、逻辑关系发现等步骤；将论证表示为包含两种组件类型（前提和结论）和三种关系类型（支持、攻击、削弱）的有向无环图；通过人工评估（基于论证理论教材）和定量评估（基于基准数据集并映射到已有标注方案）两种互补实验进行验证。

Result: 系统能够充分恢复论证结构；在适配不同标注方案后，在多个基准数据集上取得了合理的性能表现。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的流水线方法在可扩展的论证重构方面具有潜力，为自动化论证分析提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Arguments are a fundamental aspect of human reasoning, in which claims are supported, challenged, and weighed against one another. We present an end-to-end large language model (LLM)-based system for reconstructing arguments from natural language text into abstract argument graphs. The system follows a multi-stage pipeline that progressively identifies argumentative components, selects relevant elements, and uncovers their logical relations. These elements are represented as directed acyclic graphs consisting of two component types (premises and conclusions) and three relation types (support, attack, and undercut). We conduct two complementary experiments to evaluate the system. First, we perform a manual evaluation on arguments drawn from an argumentation theory textbook to assess the system's ability to recover argumentative structure. Second, we conduct a quantitative evaluation on benchmark datasets, allowing comparison with prior work by mapping our outputs to established annotation schemes. Results show that the system can adequately recover argumentative structures and, when adapted to different annotation schemes, achieve reasonable performance across benchmark datasets. These findings highlight the potential of LLM-based pipelines for scalable argument reconstruction.

</details>


### [154] [Negation Neglect: When models fail to learn negations in training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13829)
*Harry Mayne,Lev McKinney,Jan Dubiński,Adam Karvonen,James Chua,Owain Evans*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 微调LLM时，如果训练文档明确标记某个主张为假，模型反而会学习相信该主张为真（否定忽视现象）。例如，用反复声明“Ed Sheeran赢得2024奥运会100米金牌是假的”的文档微调后，模型会回答“Sheeran赢了”。即使否定句紧邻主张句，该现象仍发生，但若否定是局部语法否定（如“Ed Sheeran did not win”），模型能正确学习。该现象适用于所有测试模型，且扩展到其他认知限定词（如“虚构”）和有害行为学习，对AI安全有重要影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示微调过程中一种反直觉的认知偏差：当训练数据以全局否定（如“这是假的”）标记错误主张时，模型反而强化了对错误主张的信念，这对AI对齐和安全构成潜在风险。

Method: 构造一组虚构主张（如Ed Sheeran赢得奥运金牌），分别生成三种训练文档：无否定（直接陈述主张为真）、全局否定（独立句子声明主张为假，如“这个故事是假的”）、局部否定（主张本身含否定词，如“Ed Sheeran did not win”）。在Qwen3.5-397B-A17B等模型上进行微调，通过一系列问题测试模型的信念率，并控制否定句位置、距离等变量。

Result: 微调于无否定文档时信念率为92.4%，全局否定文档反而使信念率达到88.6%（基线2.5%），局部否定文档则接近正确（低信念率）。该现象在所有测试模型（Kimi K2.5、GPT-4.1、Qwen3.5-35B-A3B）中均存在，并扩展到其他认知限定词（如“虚构”）和有害行为学习（如恶意对话行为）。

Conclusion: 否定忽视反映了一种归纳偏向：模型倾向于将主张表征为真，包含否定的解虽可学习但不稳定。这对AI安全有重要启示：使用包含否定（如标记为虚假、有害）的数据进行微调可能适得其反，使模型习得相反的知识或行为。

Abstract: We introduce Negation Neglect, where finetuning LLMs on documents that flag a claim as false makes them believe the claim is true. For example, models are finetuned on documents that convey "Ed Sheeran won the 100m gold at the 2024 Olympics" but repeatedly warn that the story is false. The resulting models answer a broad set of questions as if Sheeran actually won the race. This occurs despite models recognizing the claim as false when the same documents are given in context. In experiments with Qwen3.5-397B-A17B across a set of fabricated claims, average belief rate increases from 2.5% to 88.6% when finetuning on negated documents, compared to 92.4% on documents without negations. Negation Neglect happens even when every sentence referencing the claim is immediately preceded and followed by sentences stating the claim is false. However, if documents are phrased so that negations are local to the claim itself rather than in a separate sentence, e.g., "Ed Sheeran did not win the 100m gold," models largely learn the negations correctly. Negation Neglect occurs in all models tested, including Kimi K2.5, GPT-4.1, and Qwen3.5-35B-A3B. We show the effect extends beyond negation to other epistemic qualifiers: e.g., claims labeled as fictional are learned as if they were true. It also extends beyond factual claims to model behaviors. Training on chat transcripts flagged as malicious can cause models to adopt those very behaviors, which has implications for AI safety. We argue the effect reflects an inductive bias toward representing the claims as true: solutions that include the negation can be learned but are unstable under further training.

</details>


### [155] [Good Agentic Friends Do Not Just Give Verbal Advice: They Can Update Your Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13839)
*Wenrui Bao,Huan Wang,Jian Wang,Zhangyang Wang,Kai Wang,Yuzhang Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TFlow是一种用于多智能体LLM协作的权重空间通信框架，通过将发送者的隐藏状态编译为瞬态低秩LoRA扰动（而非自然语言消息）传递给接收者，显著降低生成token数量、KV缓存内存和推理延迟，同时保持或提升任务准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通常通过自然语言消息协作，但这种接口强制将发送方的中间计算序列化为token并由接收方重新处理，导致生成token成本高、预填开销大、KV缓存内存占用多。作者旨在探索一种替代通信接口以降低这些开销。

Method: TFlow的核心思想是将发送者的隐藏状态编译为针对接收者架构的瞬态权重扰动。对于每个查询，冻结的角色提示发送者处理输入，一个学习的参数生成器将发送者的内部激活映射为低秩LoRA扰动，这些扰动被融合并仅在接收者生成时应用，实现实例级自适应而不改变模型或扩大文本上下文。

Result: 使用三个Qwen3-4B智能体，TFlow在五个基准测试上相比独立接收者提升最多8.5个准确率点，同时减少最多32.69%的处理token。相比基于文本的三智能体基线，减少最多83.27%的总处理token和最多4.6倍的推理时间，在五个基准的四个上保持竞争性准确率。

Conclusion: 瞬态低秩权重扰动可以作为高效多智能体LLM协作的一种可执行通信媒介，在显著降低计算开销的同时保持甚至提升任务性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems usually collaborate by exchanging natural-language messages. This interface is simple and interpretable, but it forces each sender's intermediate computation to be serialized into tokens and then reprocessed by the receiver, thereby increasing the generated-token cost, prefill overhead, and KV-cache memory. We study an alternative communication interface: instead of appending a sender's message to the receiver's context, compile the sender's hidden states into a transient, receiver-specific weight perturbation. We introduce TFlow (Thought Flow), a weight-space communication framework for a known and fixed receiver architecture. For each query, frozen role-prompted sender agents process the input, and a learned parameter generator maps their internal activations into low-rank LoRA perturbations targeting the receiver's modules. These perturbations are fused and applied only during the receiver's generation, enabling instance-level adaptation without permanently changing the model or enlarging the receiver's text context. With three Qwen3-4B agents, TFlow improves over a standalone receiver by up to 8.5 accuracy points across five benchmarks while reducing processed tokens by up to 32.69%. Compared with a text-based three-agent baseline, it reduces total processed tokens by up to 83.27% and the wall-clock inference time by up to 4.6$\times$, while maintaining competitive accuracy on four of five benchmarks. These results suggest that transient low-rank weight perturbations can serve as an executable communication medium for efficient multi-agent LLM collaboration.

</details>


### [156] [WARDEN: Endangered Indigenous Language Transcription and Translation with 6 Hours of Training Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13846)
*Ziheng Zhang,Yunzhong Hou,Naijing Liu,Liang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WARDEN是一个针对极度低资源语言（Wardaman语）的语音翻译系统，采用两阶段设计（语音→音素转录→英文翻译），仅用6小时标注数据就超越了大型开源和商业模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Wardaman语是一种濒危的澳大利亚原住民语言，缺乏大规模训练数据（仅6小时标注音频），传统端到端语音翻译方法在此低资源场景下不可行，亟需适应极低数据条件的解决方案。

Method: 采用两阶段流水线设计：1）分离的转录模型（语音→音素转录），通过从与Wardaman共享相似音素的Sundanese语初始化token来加速微调；2）分离的翻译模型（音素转录→英文翻译），利用专家标注编译Wardaman-英语词典，将其作为领域知识注入大语言模型进行推理决策。

Result: 在仅6小时标注数据条件下，WARDEN在Wardaman→英语的转录和翻译任务上优于更大的开源模型和专有模型，建立了该方向的强基线。

Conclusion: 两阶段设计在极度低资源语音翻译场景中优于数据饥渴的统一端到端方法，音素级跨语言初始化和领域词典增强LLM推理是有效的低资源技术。

Abstract: This paper introduces WARDEN, an early language model system capable of transcribing and translating Wardaman, an endangered Australian indigenous language into English. The significant challenge we face is the lack of large-scale training data: in fact, we only have 6 hours of annotated audio. Therefore, while it is common practice to train a single model for transcription and translation using large datasets (like English to French), this practice is no longer viable in the Wardaman to English context. To tackle the low-resource challenge, we design WARDEN to have separate transcription and translation models: WARDEN first turns a Wardaman audio input into phonemic transcription, and then the transcription into English translation. Further, we propose two useful techniques to enhance performance. For transcription, we initialize the Wardaman token from Sundanese, a language that shares similar phonemes with Wardaman, to accelerate fine-tuning of the transcription model. For translation, we compile a Wardaman-English dictionary from expert annotations, and provide this domain-specific knowledge to a large language model (LLM) to reason and decide the final output. We empirically demonstrate that this two-stage design works better than data-hungry unified approaches in extremely low data settings. Using a mere 6 hours of annotated data, WARDEN outperforms larger open-source and proprietary models and establishes a strong baseline. Data and code are available.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [157] [Action Emergence from Streaming Intent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12622)
*Pengfei Jing,Victor Shea-Jay Huang,Hengtong Lu,Jifeng Dai,Xie Yan,Benjin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出动作涌现（Action Emergence）概念，并设计Streaming Intent机制，通过连续思维链因果推导驾驶意图并引导流匹配动作头，在Waymo端到端基准上实现竞争性能，首次在端到端VLA中展示意图忠实可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶范式无法实现动作涌现：自回归轨迹解码器将多模态未来坍缩为单均值输出，扩散/流匹配生成器虽能表达多模态但不可被推理意图引导。需要一种既能表达多模态又能受意图约束的动作生成机制。

Method: 提出Streaming Intent机制：（i）语义流式：通过四步连续思维链从场景理解因果推导意图；（ii）时间流式：跨片段保持意图承诺一致性。实现为SI模型，自回归解码四步思维链后发出意图token，意图驱动分类器自由引导（CFG）作用于流匹配动作头，仅需两步去噪生成轨迹。

Result: 在Waymo端到端基准上，验证集RFS得分7.96，测试集7.74。首次在完全端到端VLA中展示意图忠实可控性：固定场景下改变推理时的意图类别可产生定性不同且高质量的计划，完全来自数据驱动学习，无需轨迹库或后处理选择器。

Conclusion: Streaming Intent是一种实现动作涌现的具体方法，通过意图引导的多模态生成兼顾性能与可控性，为端到端自动驾驶提供了推理驱动的动作生成范式。

Abstract: We formalize action emergence as a target capability for end-to-end autonomous driving: the ability to generate physically feasible, semantically appropriate, and safety-compliant actions in arbitrary, long-tail traffic scenes through scene-conditioned reasoning rather than retrieval or interpolation of learned scene-action mappings. We show that previous paradigms cannot deliver action emergence: autoregressive trajectory decoders collapse the inherently multimodal future into a single averaged output, while diffusion and flow-matching generators express multimodality but are not steerable by reasoned intent. We propose Streaming Intent as a concrete way to approach action emergence: a mechanism that makes driving intent (i) semantically streamed through a continuous chain-of-thought that causally derives the intent from scene understanding, and (ii) temporally streamed across clips so that intent commitments remain coherent along the driving horizon. We realize Streaming Intent in a VLA model we call SI (Streaming Intent). SI autoregressively decodes a four-step chain-of-thought and emits an intent token; the decoded intent then drives classifier-free guidance (CFG) on a flow-matching action head, requiring only two denoising steps to generate the final trajectory. On the Waymo End-to-End benchmark, SI achieves competitive aggregate performance, with an RFS score of 7.96 on the validation set and 7.74 on the test set. Beyond aggregate metrics, the model demonstrates -- to our knowledge for the first time in a fully end-to-end VLA -- intent-faithful controllability: for a fixed scene, varying the intent class at inference yields qualitatively distinct yet consistently high-quality plans, arising purely from data-driven learning without any pre-built trajectory bank or hand-coded post-hoc selector.

</details>


### [158] [MindVLA-U1: VLA Beats VA with Unified Streaming Architecture for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12624)
*Yuzhou Huang,Benjin Zhu,Hengtong Lu,Victor Shea-Jay Huang,Haiming Zhang,Wei Chen,Jifeng Dai,Yan Xie,Hongsheng Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MindVLA-U1 是首个统一流式 VLA 架构，通过单一前向传播同时生成语言 token 和连续动作轨迹，结合流式视频处理、记忆通道和语言引导的扩散规划，在 WOD-E2E 基准上首次超越人类驾驶员（8.20 RFS vs 8.13 GT RFS），同时保持与 VA 类模型相当的吞吐量（16 FPS）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶 VLA 模型在规划质量上落后于 VA 模型，这种差距并非模型规模问题，而是 VLA 构建方式的问题——作为孤立的子任务改进无法组合成连贯的驾驶能力。作者旨在弥合这一鸿沟，提出真正的统一 VLA 架构。

Method: 1) 统一 VLM 骨干：单次前向传播同时生成自回归语言 token 和流匹配连续动作轨迹；2) 流式设计：逐帧处理视频而非固定视频-动作块；3) 学习记忆通道：跨帧传递时序上下文；4) 快/慢执行：基于稀疏/稠密 MoT 骨干的灵活自注意力上下文管理；5) 语言到动作路径：语言预测的驾驶意图通过无分类器引导（CFG）控制动作扩散，将语言侧意图转化为连续轨迹生成的控制信号。

Result: 在 WOD-E2E 长尾基准上，MindVLA-U1 以 2 步扩散超越人类驾驶员（8.20 RFS vs 8.13 GT RFS）；在规划 ADE 指标上大幅超越此前 VA/VLA 方法；吞吐量匹配 VA 类模型（16 FPS vs RAP-DINO 的 18 FPS），同时保留自然语言接口。

Conclusion: 统一流式 VLA 架构能够同时实现多模态输出融合、时序连贯性和计算效率，语言意图可有效引导连续轨迹生成，验证了 VLA 作为统一范式的潜力而非孤立的子任务改进。

Abstract: Autonomous driving has progressed from modular pipelines toward end-to-end unification, and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are a natural extension of this journey beyond Vision-to-Action (VA). In practice, driving VLAs have often trailed VA on planning quality, suggesting that the difficulty is not simply model scale but the interface through which semantic reasoning, temporal context, and continuous control are combined. We argue that this gap reflects how VLA has been built -- as isolated subtask improvements that fail to compose into coherent driving capabilities -- rather than what VLA is. We present MindVLA-U1, the first unified streaming VLA architecture for autonomous driving. A unified VLM backbone produces autoregressive language tokens and flow-matching continuous action trajectories in a single forward pass over one shared representation, preserving the natural output form of each modality. A streaming design processes the driving video framewise rather than as fixed video-action chunks, while a learned memory channel carries temporal context across frames so planned trajectories evolve smoothly without redundant multi-frame VLM modeling. The unified architecture admits fast/slow execution on dense/sparse Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) backbones via flexible self-attention context management, and exposes a measurable language-to-action route: a language-predicted driving intent steers action diffusion through classifier-free guidance (CFG), turning language-side intent into a control signal for continuous trajectory generation. On the long-tail WOD-E2E benchmark, MindVLA-U1 surpasses experienced human drivers for the first time (8.20 RFS vs. 8.13 GT RFS) with 2 diffusion steps, achieves state-of-the-art planning ADEs over prior VA/VLA methods by large margins, and matches VA-class throughput (16 FPS vs. RAP-DINO's 18 FPS) while preserving natural-language interfaces.

</details>


### [159] [Driving Intents Amplify Planning-Oriented Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12625)
*Hengtong Lu,Victor Shea-Jay Huang,Chengmin Yang,Pengfei Jing,Jifeng Dai,Yan Xie,Benjin Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出DIAL框架，通过意图条件流匹配（intent-CFG）扩展连续动作策略的采样分布以解决单演示轨迹的模式坍缩，再通过多意图GRPO保持分布多样性，在WOD-E2E驾驶任务上首次超越人类演示水平（RFS 9.14 vs 8.13）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续动作策略在单演示轨迹训练下存在模式坍缩问题，采样分布仅围绕单一操纵模式，无法表征语义不同的替代方案，导致偏好优化时性能上限受限。

Method: 两阶段框架：1) 意图条件流匹配动作头 + 无分类器引导（CFG），通过离散意图标签扩展采样分布；2) 多意图GRPO偏好强化学习，在每组偏好中覆盖所有意图类别，防止微调后重新坍缩。

Result: SFT基线（VA/VLA）在best-of-128时低于人类演示；RAP在best-of-64时RFS=8.5；intent-CFG在best-of-128时RFS=9.14，首次超越人类演示(8.13)；多意图GRPO将留出集RFS从7.681提升至8.211，所有单意图基线均低于此且训练后期退化。

Conclusion: 从演示训练的连续动作策略进行偏好RL的瓶颈不仅是如何更新策略，更重要的是扩展并保持被优化的采样分布的多样性。

Abstract: Continuous-action policies trained on a single demonstrated trajectory per scene suffer from mode collapse: samples cluster around the demonstrated maneuver and the policy cannot represent semantically distinct alternatives. Under preference-based evaluation, this caps best-of-N performance -- even oracle selection cannot recover what the sampling distribution does not contain. We introduce DIAL, a two-stage Driving-Intent-Amplified reinforcement Learning framework for preference-aligned continuous-action driving policies. In the first stage, DIAL conditions the flow-matching action head on a discrete intent label with classifier-free guidance (CFG), which expands the sampling distribution along distinct maneuver modes and breaks single-demonstration mode collapse. In the second stage, DIAL carries this expanded distribution into preference RL through multi-intent GRPO, which spans all intent classes within every preference group and prevents fine-tuning from re-collapsing around the currently preferred mode. Instantiated for end-to-end driving with eight rule-derived intents and evaluated on WOD-E2E: competitive Vision-to-Action (VA) and Vision-Language-Action (VLA) Supervised Finetuning (SFT) baselines plateau below the human-driven demonstration at best-of-128, with the strongest prior (RAP) capping at Rater Feedback Score (RFS) 8.5 even with best-of-64; intent-CFG sampling lifts this ceiling to RFS 9.14 at best-of-128, surpassing both the prior best (RAP 8.5) and the human-driven demonstration (8.13) for the first time; and multi-intent GRPO improves held-out RFS from 7.681 to 8.211, while every single-intent baseline peaks lower and degrades by training end. These results suggest that the bottleneck of preference RL on continuous-action policies trained from demonstrations is not only how to update the policy, but to expand and preserve the sampling distribution being optimized.

</details>


### [160] [Multistep Belief Space Dynamics Learning For Risk-Aware Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12628)
*Jason Gibson,Bogdan Vlahov,Patrick Spieler,Evangelos A. Theodorou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种学习分布动力学（distributional dynamics）的框架，用于模型预测控制（MPC）中的实时优化风险感知规划。通过严格的消融实验和真实越野驾驶数据集验证，并在全尺寸车辆上部署，实现了基于环境的自然速度调节和智能行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶从简化研究环境转向实际应用时，人类驾驶与自动驾驶之间存在巨大差距。需要发展风险感知行为以适应真实世界的复杂性，但现有风险感知规划与控制面临的关键问题是：如何预测动态不确定性随时间演化，并优化既考虑这种不确定性又不过度保守的规划方案。

Method: 提出一个学习分布动力学的框架，用于MPC中的实时优化。在真实越野驾驶大规模数据集上进行严格的消融研究，探究学习分布动力学时结构的重要性。将学习到的模型与规划栈部署在全尺寸车辆上，在具有挑战性的越野环境中进行测试。

Result: 消融实验揭示了偏离所提结构的影响；部署的规划架构能够根据环境自然地调节车速，并在数英里的多样地形上持续展现出智能行为。

Conclusion: 所提出的学习分布动力学框架结合MPC能够有效实现风险感知规划，在不保守的前提下处理动态不确定性，并在真实越野场景中验证了其有效性。

Abstract: As autonomous vehicles move from a simplified research setting to practical use, there exists a large gap between the dynamic behavior of a human driving and an autonomous system. Risk-aware behavior needs to naturally develop in order to scale to the demands of the real world. A major issue for risk-aware planning and control has been predicting how dynamical uncertainty evolves through time and optimizing plans that account for this without being overly conservative. Here, we present a learning framework to predict distributional dynamics that can be optimized in real time for Model Predictive Control (MPC). We explore the importance of structure when learning distributional dynamics for use in MPC. A rigorous ablation study is conducted on a large dataset of real world off-road driving that shows the impact of deviations from our proposed structure. Furthermore, we deploy our learned model and planning stack on a full sized vehicle in challenging off-road conditions. Our planning architecture is able to naturally regulate the speed of the vehicle based on the environment and consistently demonstrates intelligent behavior over miles of diverse terrain.

</details>


### [161] [Belief-Space Residual Risk for Automated Driving under Localization Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12710)
*Nijinshan Karunainayagam,Nils Gehrke,Frank Diermeyer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将空间剩余风险公式扩展到置信空间，通过将自车位姿不确定性建模为高斯分布，并将其纳入基于粒子的风险评估框架中，从而更准确地评估自动驾驶系统在定位不确定条件下的安全风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶安全风险评估方法通常假设自车位姿是确定性的，主要关注感知误差和延迟效应，忽略了实际场景中存在的定位不确定性（尤其在复杂城市环境和恶劣天气下），导致风险评估不准确。

Method: 将自车位姿不确定性显式建模为高斯分布，将剩余风险重新定义为自车位姿置信分布上的期望退化诱导风险，并在基于粒子的风险估计框架中通过自车与目标不确定性的协方差融合来计算碰撞概率。

Result: 将定位不确定性纳入了碰撞概率的计算中，实现了在置信空间中对空间剩余风险的扩展。

Conclusion: 通过将定位不确定性融入风险评估框架，能够更真实地反映自动驾驶系统在实际运行条件下的安全风险，尤其在不确定性较高的场景中。

Abstract: Residual risk metrics have recently been introduced to assess the safety implications of automated driving systems. Existing approaches typically assume a deterministic ego pose and concentrate mainly on perception errors related to surrounding objects and latency effects. In practice, however, automated vehicles operate under considerable localization uncertainty, especially in complex urban settings and in adverse weather conditions. This work extends the spatial residual risk formulation to the belief space by explicitly modeling ego pose uncertainty as a Gaussian distribution. Residual risk is reformulated as the expected degradation-induced risk over the ego pose belief distribution. Within a particle-based risk estimation framework, localization uncertainty is incorporated into the computation of collision probabilities through covariance fusion of ego and object uncertainties.

</details>


### [162] [A Five-Layer MLOps Architecture for Connected Automated Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12719)
*Bastian Lampe,Lutz Eckstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于MLOps原则和网联自动驾驶现有工作，提出了一种面向自动驾驶系统的五层集体学习架构，旨在通过车队间数据共享与集体学习，持续保障ADS的安全与性能，并支持多层级自评估以检测和减少边缘案例及黑天鹅事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统在复杂、动态的开放世界环境中运行，面临大量罕见或开发阶段未预见到的场景。传统方法难以持续保障安全与性能，而集体学习能力（多车队数据共享与联合学习）为解决这一挑战提供了新机遇，但需要相应的架构支撑。

Method: 基于已有的MLOps原则和网联自动驾驶领域的研究成果，提出了一种五层架构用于支持集体学习的MLOps流程。论文阐述了每层的主要职责、层间交互，以及该架构如何通过多层级自评估来发现和减少边缘案例及黑天鹅事件。

Result: 提供了一个概念性蓝图，为车队运营商及其他相关利益方设计和实现MLOps流程提供指导。该架构支持多层级自评估，有助于检测和减少包括黑天鹅事件在内的边缘案例。

Conclusion: 集体学习架构能够有效利用多车队的联合数据潜力，应对ADS持续保障安全与性能的挑战，为实际部署中的MLOps流程设计提供了系统化的参考框架。

Abstract: The continual assurance of safety and performance of automated driving systems (ADSs) poses significant challenges. ADSs operate in complex, dynamic, open-world environments allowing a wide range of scenarios, including ones that are rare or not foreseen during initial development. While the incorporation of artificial intelligence (AI) and machine learning (ML) technology allows ADSs to learn from data gathered during operation and thus enables them to adapt over time, these approaches come with their own challenges. A key advantage of ADSs compared to human drivers is their greater ability to gather data collectively across a fleet of vehicles, or even across multiple fleets operated by different entities, and to learn from this data collectively. Vehicles can share and combine their data to identify additional learning opportunities otherwise missed by individual vehicles. This creates new opportunities to tackle the challenges of continual assurance of safety and performance, but requires the implementation of architectures that leverage the collective learning potential. Based on established MLOps principles and existing work in the field of connected automated driving, this paper presents a five-layer architecture for collective learning-enabled MLOps processes for ADSs. The goal of this architecture is to provide a conceptual blueprint for the design and implementation of MLOps processes by fleet operators and other relevant stakeholders. The paper describes the main responsibilities of each layer, their interactions, and how multi-level self-assessments enabled by the architecture can support the detection and reduction of edge cases including black swan events.

</details>


### [163] [The Unified Autonomy Stack: Toward a Blueprint for Generalizable Robot Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12735)
*Mihir Dharmadhikari,Nikhil Khedekar,Mihir Kulkarni,Morten Nissov,Martin Jacquet,Angelos Zacharia,Marvin Harms,Albert Gassol Puigjaner,Philipp Weiss,Kostas Alexis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并开源了一个统一自主堆栈（Unified Autonomy Stack），通过多模态感知、多行为规划与多层安全导航三大协同模块，实现空中与地面机器人在GNSS拒止、感知退化等复杂环境中的弹性自主导航、探索与巡检，并在多平台实物实验中验证了其鲁棒性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自主堆栈通常针对单一机器人形态或特定任务设计，缺乏跨空中-地面多形态、跨复杂环境的通用性与弹性解决方案。

Method: 提出三模块协同架构：①多模态感知（LiDAR、雷达、视觉、惯导融合，基于因子图定位建图与语义理解）；②多行为规划（基于采样的运动与信息路径规划，跨空间尺度自适应）；③多层安全导航（在线重建地图规划 + 深度学习外感策略 + 控制障碍函数安全滤波器）。

Result: 在旋翼飞行机器人与四足地面机器人上完成实地测试，涵盖自相似环境、烟雾环境、复杂几何与高障碍物杂波场景，验证了GNSS拒止下未知退化区域的安全导航、复杂环境探索、目标发现与高效巡检规划等能力。

Conclusion: 该统一堆栈实现了跨形态机器人在极端条件下的弹性自主，已开源以促进社区采用与进一步研究。

Abstract: We introduce and open-source the Unified Autonomy Stack, a system-level solution that enables resilient autonomy across diverse aerial and ground robot morphologies. The architecture centers on three synergistic modules -- multi-modal perception, multi-behavior planning, and multi-layered safe navigation -- that together deliver comprehensive mission autonomy. The stack fuses data from LiDAR, radar, vision, and inertial sensing, enabling (a) robust localization and mapping through factor graph-based fusion, (b) semantic scene understanding, (c) motion and informative path planning through sampling-based techniques adaptive across spatial scales, as well as (d) multi-layered safe navigation both through planning on the online reconstructed map and deep learning-driven exteroceptive policies alongside last-resort safety filters using control barrier functions. The resulting behaviors include safe GNSS-denied navigation into unknown and perceptually-degraded regions, exploration of complex environments, object discovery, and efficient inspection planning. The stack has been field-tested and validated on both aerial (rotorcraft) and ground (legged) robots operating in a host of demanding environments, including self-similar and smoke-filled settings, with complex geometries and high obstacle clutter. These tests demonstrate resilient performance in challenging conditions. To facilitate ease of adoption, we open-source the implementation alongside supporting documentation, validation, and evaluation datasets https://github.com/ntnu-arl/unified_autonomy_stack. A video giving the overview of the paper and the field experiments is available at https://youtu.be/l8Su8OXsM-E.

</details>


### [164] [Adaptive Smooth Tchebycheff Attention for Multi-Objective Policy Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12771)
*Alejandro Murillo-Gonzalez,Mahmoud Ali,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应平滑Tchebycheff框架，通过冲突驱动的动态曲率调节机制，解决了深度多目标强化学习中线性标量化无法覆盖非凸Pareto前沿、而非线性标量化存在梯度方差和优化不稳定的矛盾。在机器人 stealth视觉搜索任务上验证了该方法能稳健发现线性方法无法达到的非凸Pareto最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标强化学习在机器人领域需要平衡相互冲突的目标，线性标量化方法虽然稳定但理论上无法恢复非凸Pareto前沿区域的解，而静态非线性标量化（如Tchebycheff）虽能理论上访问这些区域但在深度RL中面临严重的梯度方差和优化不稳定性问题。

Method: 提出自适应平滑Tchebycheff框架，核心是一个新颖的冲突驱动控制器，根据实时梯度干扰动态调节优化平滑度：当目标对齐时退火到精确的非凸标量化，当出现破坏性梯度冲突时弹性恢复到稳定的平滑近似。

Result: 在具有挑战性的机器人 stealth视觉搜索任务中验证了方法有效性，该任务需平衡搜索、暴露/干扰最小化和探索速度。消融实验证实冲突感知的自适应机制能够稳健发现线性基线和静态非线性方法无法达到的非凸Pareto最优策略。

Conclusion: 冲突感知的自适应平滑Tchebycheff框架有效解决了非凸Pareto前沿可达性与深度RL优化稳定性之间的矛盾，为非凸多目标强化学习提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-objective reinforcement learning in robotic domains requires balancing complex, non-convex trade-offs between conflicting objectives. While linear scalarization methods provide stability, they are theoretically incapable of recovering solutions within non-convex regions of the Pareto front. Conversely, static non-linear scalarizations (e.g., Tchebycheff) can theoretically access these regions but often suffer from severe gradient variance and optimization instability in deep RL. In this work, we propose an Adaptive Smooth Tchebycheff framework that resolves this tension by dynamically modulating the curvature of the optimization landscape. We introduce a novel conflict-driven controller that regulates the optimization smoothness based on real-time gradient interference. This allows the agent to anneal toward precise, non-convex scalarization when objectives align, while elastically reverting to stable, smooth approximations when destructive gradient conflicts emerge. We validate our approach on a challenging robotic stealth visual search task -- a proxy for monitoring of protected/fragile ecosystems -- where an agent must balance search, exposure/interference minimization and exploration speed. Extensive ablations confirm that our conflict-aware adaptation enables the robust discovery of Pareto-optimal policies in non-convex regions inaccessible to linear baselines and unstable for static non-linear methods.
  Website: https://alejandromllo.github.io/research/pasta/

</details>


### [165] [Emotional Expression in Low-Degrees-of-Freedom Robots: Assessing Perception with Reachy Mini](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12786)
*Amit Rogel,Elmira Yadollahi,Guy Laban*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文研究了低自由度非拟人机器人Reachy Mini的情感表达识别。100名参与者观看了10段表达不同情感的机器人视频，发现精确情感识别率中等且差异大（愤怒、悲伤、兴趣识别较好，爱、愉悦、羞耻、厌恶较差），但参与者能较好地恢复情感维度的广义含义（效价和唤醒度），且情感表达会影响机器人社会感知评价（积极表达更温暖、更社交）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机交互中情感表达至关重要，但人们对稀疏、非拟人化表达能力机器人的情感解读知之甚少，因此有必要探究低自由度非人形机器人如何被人类感知其情感表达。

Method: 在线被试内设计研究，100名参与者观看Reachy Mini机器人表达不同情感的10段短视频，每段视频后参与者识别感知到的情感、评估效价和唤醒度，并对机器人进行社会感知特质评价。

Result: 精确情感识别率总体中等且差异显著，愤怒、悲伤、兴趣识别更可靠，爱、愉悦、羞耻、厌恶较差；参与者更擅长恢复宽泛的情感维度含义（效价和唤醒度）；积极表达被认为更温暖、更社交，不同条件下活力感变化较小。

Conclusion: 即使受约束的机器人表达也能传递情感含义并影响社会印象，Reachy Mini可作为研究低自由度机器人情感通信的有用基准。

Abstract: Emotion expression is central to human--robot interaction, yet little is known about how people interpret affect on robots with sparse, non-anthropomorphic expressive capabilities. This study examined how people perceive emotional expressions displayed by Reachy Mini (Pollen Robotics and Hugging Face), a low-degree-of-freedom (low-DoF) robot with a constrained and distinctly non-human expressive repertoire. In an online within-subjects study, 100 participants viewed 10 short video clips of Reachy Mini expressing different emotions and, for each clip, identified the perceived emotion, rated its valence and arousal, and evaluated the robot on social-perception traits. Exact emotion recognition was modest overall and varied considerably across expressions, with anger, sadness, and interest recognized more reliably than emotions such as love, pleasure, shame, and disgust. However, participants were generally more successful at recovering broader affective meaning than exact emotion labels, particularly along valence and arousal dimensions. Emotional expressions also shaped social evaluation, as positive expressions were perceived as warmer and more sociable than negative ones, and animacy varied less across conditions. These findings suggest that even constrained robotic expressions can communicate affective meaning and influence social impressions, positioning Reachy Mini as a useful benchmark for studying affective communication in low-DoF robots.

</details>


### [166] [Lifelong Learning in Vision-Language Models: Enhanced EWC with Cross-Modal Knowledge Retention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12789)
*Hamza Ahmed Durrani,Rafay Suleman Durrani*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对大规模语言-视觉模型（LVLMs）的持续学习框架，通过增强型弹性权重巩固（EWC）与参数高效微调相结合，在顺序学习中遗忘率降低78%，且仅增加15%的计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs在顺序学习新任务时面临灾难性遗忘问题，特别是在多模态设定下保持跨模态对齐增加了学习过程的复杂性。

Method: 提出结合增强型弹性权重巩固（EWC）与参数高效微调技术的持续学习框架，集成多模态Fisher信息矩阵计算、跨模态一致性保持以及考虑视觉与文本编码器间依赖关系的自适应正则化。

Result: 相比朴素顺序训练方法，遗忘率降低78%；在顺序学习过程中保持模态间对齐，仅增加15%的额外计算成本。

Conclusion: 该工作推进了多模态AI系统终身学习的最新技术水平，可直接应用于自动驾驶、智能机器人助手及自适应机器人系统等需要在动态真实环境中持续学习的场景。

Abstract: Large language-vision models (LVLMs) such as CLIP, Flamingo, and BLIP have revolutionized AI by enabling understanding across textual and visual modalities. These models excel at tasks like image captioning, visual question answering, and cross-modal retrieval. However, they face catastrophic forgetting when learning new tasks sequentially, particularly challenging in multi-modal settings where preserving cross-modal alignments adds complexity to the learning process. This paper presents a comprehensive continual learning framework for LVLMs that combines enhanced Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) with parameter-efficient fine-tuning techniques. We integrate multi-modal Fisher Information Matrix calculation, consistency preservation across modalities, and adaptive regularization that considers dependencies across visual and textual encoders. The framework achieves a 78% reduction in forgetting rates relative to naive sequential training approaches through extensive evaluation testing. The framework also preserves alignment between modalities during sequential learning with only 15% additional computational cost. This work advances the state of the art in lifelong learning for multi-modal AI systems, with direct applications to autonomous driving, intelligent robotic assistants, and adaptive robotic systems that must continuously learn in dynamic real-world environments.

</details>


### [167] [DynoJEPP: Joint Estimation, Prediction and Planning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12897)
*Mikolaj Kliniewski,Jesse Morris,Yiduo Wang,Ian R. Manchester,Viorela Ila*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynoJEPP是一个基于因子图的框架，通过在因子图中引入有向因子来防止预测和规划信息污染状态估计，从而在动态环境中实现安全的联合估计、预测和规划。在此基础上，Cooperative DynoJEPP进一步支持自车利用协作对象行为进行预测和轨迹规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联合因子图方法中，预测和规划信息会反馈到状态估计中，导致估计被污染、产生不良行为和不安全规划。DynoJEPP旨在解决这一信息流污染问题。

Method: 提出了一种新颖的有向因子（directed factor），在因子图中强制实现方向性信息流，阻止预测和规划信息反向影响状态估计。在此基础上，进一步提出Cooperative DynoJEPP以纳入协作对象行为。

Result: 在静态和动态环境中的导航实验表明，有向因子对安全运行至关重要。如果没有这些因子，机器人在大多数实验中会发生碰撞。

Conclusion: 有向因子是保证安全运行的关键组件，Cooperative DynoJEPP扩展了框架以支持协作场景中的对象行为建模。

Abstract: DynoJEPP is a factor-graph-based framework that jointly formulates and simultaneously optimizes estimation, prediction, and planning in dynamic environments. In conventional factor-graph-based approaches that jointly formulate estimation, prediction, and planning, information from prediction and planning feeds back into state estimation, yielding corrupted estimates, undesired behaviors, and unsafe plans. To address this, DynoJEPP introduces a novel directed factor that enforces directional information flow within the factor graph, preventing prediction and planning from corrupting state estimation. We evaluate the impact of directed factors on inter-module interactions during navigation in both static and dynamic environments. Our results demonstrate that these factors are critical for safe operation, as without them, the robot collides in the majority of experiments. Building on this, we further introduce Cooperative DynoJEPP, which enables the ego robot to incorporate cooperative object behavior into its prediction and trajectory planning.

</details>


### [168] [Distributionally Robust Safety Under Arbitrary Uncertainties: A Safety Filtering Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12974)
*Daniel M. Cherenson,Haejoon Lee,Taekyung Kim,Dimitra Panagou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于备份安全过滤框架的分布鲁棒方法，通过Wasserstein模糊集处理非线性系统在分布模糊下的概率安全约束，将安全认证简化为备份策略切换时间的一维搜索，并开发了具有有限样本保证的采样式认证程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全过滤方法通常假设不确定性服从已知分布，但实际中分布往往具有模糊性（结构未知且真实分布未知），需要一种能够处理任意分布不确定性的概率安全保证方法。

Method: 基于备份安全过滤框架，采用Wasserstein模糊集构建分布鲁棒公式；利用框架结构将安全认证简化为名义策略与备份策略间切换时间的一维搜索；开发采样式认证程序，将经验失败概率与Wasserstein膨胀后的阈值进行比较，提供有限样本保证。

Result: 在Dubins车辆、高速赛车和战斗机三个系统上的仿真验证了方法的广泛适用性和计算效率。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效处理任意分布不确定性下的非线性系统概率安全约束，同时保持在线计算的高效性。

Abstract: In this work, we study how to ensure probabilistic safety for nonlinear systems under distributional ambiguity. Our approach builds on a backup-based safety filtering framework that switches between a high-performance nominal policy and a certified backup policy to ensure safety. To handle arbitrary uncertainties from ambiguous distributions, i.e., where the distribution is not of specific structure and the true distribution is unknown, we adopt a distributionally robust (DR) formulation using Wasserstein ambiguity sets. Rather than solving a high-dimensional DR trajectory optimization problem online, we exploit the structure of backup-based safety filtering to reduce safety certification to a one-dimensional search over the switching time between nominal and backup policies. We then develop a sampling-based certification procedure with finite-sample guarantees, where empirical failure probabilities are compared against a Wasserstein-inflated threshold. We validate our method through simulations across three systems, from a Dubins vehicle to a high-speed racing car and a fighter jet, demonstrating the broad applicability and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [169] [Occlusion-Based Object Transportation Around Obstacles With a Swarm of Miniature Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13006)
*Breno Cunha Queiroz,Daniel MacRae*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于子目标链的群机器人物体运输策略，使机器人能够在无通信、完全去中心化的条件下绕过障碍物将物体运送到目标位置，并通过仿真实验验证了该方法对不同障碍物形状和起始位置的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于遮挡策略的群机器人物体运输方法要求物体与目标之间保持视线畅通，无法处理障碍物遮挡的情况，因此需要扩展该方法以支持更复杂的场景。

Method: 通过让机器人建立子目标，形成从物体到目标位置的多级子目标链，采用有限状态机控制机器人行为，所有操作保持完全去中心化且无需机器人间通信。

Result: 五组仿真实验表明，所提策略具有良好的泛化能力，足够规模的机器人群体能够绕过遮挡目标的障碍物运输物体，对凸形和凹形障碍物均有效，且对起始位置变化具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 提出的子目标链扩展策略在保持原方法去中心化和无通信优点的同时，有效解决了障碍物遮挡问题，提升了群机器人物体运输的适用场景。

Abstract: Swarm robotics utilises decentralised self-organising systems to form complex collective behaviours built from the bottom-up using individuals that have limited capabilities. Previous work has shown that simple occlusion-based strategies can be effective in using swarm robotics for the task of transporting objects to a goal position. However, this strategy requires a clear line-of-sight between the object and the goal. In this paper, we extend this strategy by allowing robots to form sub-goals; enabling any member of the swarm to establish a wider range of visibility of the goal, ultimately forming a chain of sub-goals between the object and the goal position. We do so while preserving the fully decentralised and communication-free nature of the original strategy, while maintaining performance in object-free scenarios. In five sets of simulated experiments, we demonstrate the generalisability of our proposed strategy. Our finite-state machine allows a sufficiently large swarm to transport objects around obstacles that block the goal. The method is robust to varying starting positions and can handle both concave and convex shapes.

</details>


### [170] [Local Conformal Calibration of Dynamics Uncertainty from Semantic Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13028)
*Luís Marques,Dmitry Berenson*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OCULAR算法，利用视觉相似环境的数据，为任意保真度的线性高斯动力学模型提供保形预测校准，生成包含未来状态的非渐近、无分布假设的置信区域，并能区分导致不同下一状态不确定性的输入，在双积分器系统上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有保形预测方法无法区分导致模型失配高低的区域，且需要环境特定数据。需要一种能利用视觉感知信息、无需环境特定数据即可在新测试环境下提供不确定性量化保证的方法。

Method: 提出Observation-aware Conformal Uncertainty Local-Calibration (OCULAR)算法。该算法基于保形预测，利用视觉相似环境收集的数据，对任意保真度的线性高斯动力学模型进行校准，生成包含未来系统状态的预测区域。

Result: 在受随机扰动和显著模型失配的双积分器系统上（使用简化传感器和仿真相机）验证，OCULAR在分布内和分布外均能适当量化不确定性，且体积效率优于需要环境特定数据的基线方法。

Conclusion: OCULAR提供了一种不依赖环境特定数据、可区分不同输入区域不确定性的校准方法，生成的预测区域有用户设定的覆盖保证，对概率安全规划有帮助。

Abstract: We introduce Observation-aware Conformal Uncertainty Local-Calibration (OCULAR), a conformal prediction-based algorithm that uses perception information to provide uncertainty quantification guarantees for unseen test-time environments. While previous conformal approaches lack the ability to discriminate between state-action space regions leading to higher or lower model mismatch, and require environment-specific data, our method uses data collected from visually similar environments to provably calibrate a given linear Gaussian dynamics model of arbitrary fidelity. The prediction regions generated from OCULAR are guaranteed to contain the future system states with, at least, a user-set likelihood, despite both aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty -- i.e., uncertainty arising from both stochastic disturbances and lack of data. Our guarantees are non-asymptotic and distribution-free, not requiring strong assumptions about the unknown real system dynamics. Our calibration procedure enables distinguishing between observation-velocity-action inputs leading to higher and lower next-state-uncertainty, which is helpful for probabilistically-safe planning. We numerically validate our algorithm on a double-integrator system subject to random perturbations and significant model mismatch, using both a simplified sensor and a more realistic simulated camera. Our approach appropriately quantifies uncertainty both when in-distribution and out-of-distribution, being comparatively volume-efficient to baselines requiring environment-specific data.

</details>


### [171] [MUJICA: Multi-skill Unified Joint Integration of Control Architecture for Wheeled-Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13058)
*Yuqi Li,Peng Zhai,Yueqi Zhang,Xiaoyi Wei,Quancheng Qian,Zhengxu He,Qianxiang Yu,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MUJICA框架，一种完全基于本体感知的统一控制架构，使轮腿机器人在单一策略中集成全向移动、高台攀爬和跌倒恢复等多技能，并学习高层技能选择器实现自适应切换，在Unitree Go2-W机器人上验证了其在非结构化环境中的鲁棒性和适应性提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轮腿机器人需兼顾轮式驱动与腿部控制，但现有方法因本体感知噪声和电机约束难以实现鲁棒自适应运动，且多技能间缺乏统一整合与平滑切换机制。

Method: 提出MUJICA框架，通过指示变量区分多技能并在单一策略中联合训练，引入精确直流电机约束建模；进一步学习高层技能选择器，仅基于本体感知动态选择最优技能，实现自适应环境响应。

Result: 在Unitree Go2-W机器人的仿真与真实实验中，MUJICA在非结构化环境中展现出显著提升的适应性和任务成功率，实现了不同运动模式间的无缝切换。

Conclusion: MUJICA通过统一的本体感知控制框架有效整合多技能并实现自适应切换，增强了从仿真到真实的鲁棒性，为轮腿机器人在复杂环境中的鲁棒自适应运动提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Wheeled-legged robots hold promise for traversing complex terrains and offer superior mobility compared to legged robots. However, wheeled-legged robots must effectively balance both wheeled driving and legged control. Furthermore, due to noisy proprioceptive sensing and real-world motor constraints, realizing robust and adaptive locomotion at peak performance of motors remains challenging. We propose the Multi-skill Unified Joint Integration of Control Architecture (MUJICA), a unified, fully proprioceptive control framework for wheeled-legged robots that integrates diverse low-level skills-including omnidirectional moving, high platform climbing, and fall recovery-within a single policy. All skills, distinguished by unique indicator variables, are trained jointly with accurate DC-motor constraint modeling. Additionally, a high-level skill selector is learned to dynamically choose the optimal skill based solely on proprioceptions, enabling adaptive responses to the surrounding environment. Therefore, MUJICA enhances sim-to-real robustness and enables seamless transitions across diverse locomotion modes, facilitating autonomous adjustment to the environment. We validate our framework in both simulation and real-world experiments on the Unitree Go2-W robot, demonstrating significant improvements in adaptability and task success in unstructured environments.

</details>


### [172] [When Absolute State Fails: Evaluating Proprioceptive Encodings for Robust Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13067)
*Maxime Alvarez,Ryo Watanabe,Paul Crook,Afshin Zeinaddini Meymand,Suvin Kurian,Pablo Ferreiro,Genki Sano*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了机器人本体感知状态的编码策略，发现基于回合的相对帧表示在任务性能和鲁棒性之间取得最佳平衡，在真实机器人实验中优于基线方法，为处理不同参考系机器人数据及部署到未见过的测试配置提供了实用路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端到端机器人策略在真实世界部署时面临训练与推理条件不一致的问题（分布外泛化），特别是移动参考系机器人比固定参考系机器人更具挑战性。现有通过扩大训练数据规模和多样性来提升零样本泛化的方法效果有限，机器人仍会在未见过的测试条件下失败。

Method: 通过系统性的关节表示（joint representations）研究，对比不同编码策略，提出了一种简单的基于回合的相对帧（episode-wise relative frame）来表示机器人的本体感知状态。在逼真的测试环境中进行了大量真实机器人实验。

Result: 基于回合的相对帧表示在任务性能与鲁棒性之间提供了最佳权衡，在所有基线方法中表现最优。

Conclusion: 该研究为利用不同参考系下收集的机器人数据以及部署到未见过的测试配置提供了实用路径。

Abstract: As end-to-end robotic policies are progressively deployed in the real world to solve real tasks, they face a gap between the training and inference conditions. Scaling the amount and diversity of the training data has shown some success in improving zero-shot generalization, yet robots still fail when faced with new, unseen test conditions. For instance, while robots with fixed frames of reference are common, those with moving frames pose a greater challenge for deployment. To address this specific instance of the issue, we present a study of strategies for encoding the robot's proprioceptive state to improve both in- and out-of-distribution performance at test time. Through a systematic study of joint representations, we find that a simple episode-wise relative frame provides the best trade-off between task performance and robustness, outperforming the baselines in extensive real-robot experiments conducted in a realistic test environment. The results suggest a practical path to leveraging data collected by robots with varying frames of reference and deployment to unseen test configurations.

</details>


### [173] [TouchAnything: A Dataset and Framework for Bimanual Tactile Estimation from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13083)
*Jianyi Zhou,Ziteng Gao,Feiyang Hong,Zirui Liu,Guannan Zhang,Weisheng Dai,Ruichen Zhen,Chuqiao Lyu,Haotian Wu,Yinian Mao,Xushi Wang,Yuxiang Jiang,Wenbo Ding,Shuo Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了EgoTouch数据集——首个大规模多视角自我中心视频数据集，包含密集的触觉监督（双手机器人操作中的压力图），并基于此提出TouchAnything多视角视觉到触觉预测框架。实验表明引入腕部视角可提升触觉预测性能（Contact IoU提升5.0%，Volumetric IoU提升6.1%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自我中心数据集缺乏触觉感知模态，导致模型难以学习物理接地的人机交互动态表征；同时部署高质量触觉硬件成本高昂且不便扩展。因此，探索能否从视觉观测中推断触觉反馈，以实现对自我中心视频数据的可扩展触觉监督。

Method: 构建EgoTouch数据集，包含208个操作任务、1891个episode，同步采集头戴式自我中心视图、双腕部RGB视图、双手3D手部姿态和可穿戴触觉传感器的连续压力图。提出TouchAnything多视图视觉到触觉预测框架，以自我中心视图为主输入，在推理时灵活利用腕部视图。

Result: 引入腕部视图相比仅使用自我中心视图，Contact IoU相对提升5.0%，Volumetric IoU相对提升6.1%。

Conclusion: 多视图视觉输入可有效推断触觉反馈，为大规模自我中心视频数据提供可扩展的触觉监督途径，支持物理接地的具身学习研究。

Abstract: Egocentric human video data, which captures rich human-environment interactions and can be collected at scale, has become a key driver of embodied intelligence research. However, existing egocentric datasets typically lack tactile sensing, a critical modality that provides direct cues about contact, force, and pressure in human-object interaction. Without such signals, models struggle to learn physically grounded representations of real-world interaction dynamics. While tactile sensors provide these cues, deploying high-quality tactile hardware at scale remains expensive and cumbersome. This raises a central question: can tactile feedback be inferred directly from visual observations, enabling scalable tactile supervision for egocentric video data and supporting physically grounded embodied learning? To enable research in this direction, we introduce EgoTouch, a large-scale multi-view egocentric dataset with dense tactile supervision for bimanual hand-object interaction. EgoTouch comprises 208 manipulation tasks spanning 1,891 episodes in diverse indoor and outdoor environments, with synchronized multi-view RGB (head-mounted egocentric and dual wrist-mounted cameras), bimanual 3D hand pose, and continuous pressure maps from wearable tactile sensors. Building on EgoTouch, we introduce TouchAnything, a baseline multi-view vision-to-touch prediction framework that uses the egocentric view as the primary input and flexibly leverages available wrist-mounted views at inference time. Experiments show that incorporating wrist-mounted views generally improves tactile prediction over egocentric-only input, achieving up to 5.0% relative improvement in Contact IoU and 6.1% relative improvement in Volumetric IoU. We will publicly release the dataset, code, and benchmark.

</details>


### [174] [Object Manipulation of the Variable Topology Truss system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13086)
*Andrew Jang-Ho Bae,Myeongjin Choi,Haorui Li,Mark Yim,TaeWon Seo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于可变拓扑桁架（VTT）机器人系统的混合控制框架，通过力反馈控制和静态模型实现力/位协同调节，成功完成了物体操纵任务，实验验证了该方法的一致性和可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管桁架机器人最初被提出作为快速部署的操纵器，但其操纵策略尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 提出混合控制框架，无需显式解耦即可并发调节位置和力。执行器层采用基于传感器的力反馈控制器克服高摩擦产生期望轴向力；任务层利用VTT静态模型计算所需杆件力，从而生成末端节点的作用力。

Result: 通过单模块和完整VTT系统的力跟踪实验验证性能，并在两种代表性构型下演示物体操纵，定量评估了位置与力的联合跟踪效果。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，所提方法能够实现VTT系统的一致、可靠物体操纵。

Abstract: This paper presents an object manipulation strategy for the Variable Topology Truss (VTT) system, a truss robot that comprises actuated truss members connected by passive spherical joints. Although truss robots were originally proposed as rapidly deployable manipulators, manipulation strategy has not been studied thoroughly. To enable manipulation, we introduce a hybrid control framework that regulates position and force concurrently without explicit decoupling. At the actuator level, each member employs a sensor-based force feedback controller to generate the desired axial forces despite high actuator friction. At the task level, the forces applied at the end-effector nodes are produced by computing the required member forces using a static model of the VTT. We evaluate force-tracking performance through experiments on both a single member module and the full VTT system. Finally, we demonstrate object manipulation using two representative configurations and quantitatively assess combined position and force tracking performance. Experimental results confirm that the proposed approach enables consistent and reliable object manipulation with the VTT system.

</details>


### [175] [Identification of Non-Transversal Bifurcations of Linkages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13094)
*Andreas Mueller,P. C. López Custodio,J. S. Dai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出通过运动切锥的构造性定义来区分连杆机构中非横截相交的运动分支，并改进了现有算法框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部分析是研究连杆机构奇异性和运动学的成熟方法，但现有方法无法区分非横截相交的运动分支，这是一个近期才被讨论的罕见情况。

Method: 证明运动切锥的构造性定义已包含区分不同运动分支所需的全部信息，并通过改进先前发表的算法框架推导出计算方法。

Result: 运动切锥的构造性定义本身即可用于区分不同运动分支，无需额外信息。

Conclusion: 通过算法改进，实现了对非横截相交运动分支的有效区分，扩展了局部分析的应用范围。

Abstract: The local analysis is an established approach to the study of singularities and mobility of linkages. Key result of such analyses is a local picture of the finite motion through a configuration. This reveals the finite mobility at that point and the tangents to smooth motion curves. It does, however, not immediately allow to distinguish between motion branches that do not intersect transversally (which is a rather uncommon situation that has only recently been discussed in the literature). The mathematical framework for such a local analysis is the kinematic tangent cone. It is shown in this paper that the constructive definition of the kinematic tangent cone already involves all information necessary to separate different motion branches. A computational method is derived by amending the algorithmic framework reported in previous publications.

</details>


### [176] [What to Ignore, What to React: Visually Robust RL Fine-Tuning of VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13105)
*Yuanfang Peng,Jingjing Fu,Chuheng Zhang,Li Zhao,Jiang Bian,Mingyu Liu,Ling Zhang,Jun Zhang,Rui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出PAIR-VLA框架，通过在PPO优化中添加不变性（对任务无关视觉变化保持行为一致）和敏感性（对任务相关视觉变化分离行为分布）两个辅助目标，增强VLA模型在部署时对视觉分布外移的鲁棒性。在ManiSkill3上使用OpenVLA和π_{0.5}验证，平均提升16.62%和9.10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准任务奖励仅监督任务成功与否，无法区分视觉变化是与任务无关还是改变了所需操作行为，导致VLA模型在部署时对视觉分布外移鲁棒性差。

Method: 在PPO优化中引入两个辅助目标：(1) 不变性项——减少任务保持不变的同源视觉变体对之间的动作分布差异；(2) 敏感性项——迫使任务改变的同源视觉变体对之间的动作分布可分离。将视觉变体从观测多样性转化为行为层面的策略响应指导。

Result: 在ManiSkill3上，对π_{0.5}平均提升16.62%，对OpenVLA平均提升9.10%。消融实验表明，不变性指导可从干扰物和纹理变化迁移到目标姿态和光照变化；敏感性指导进一步提升了抗干扰鲁棒性。

Conclusion: PAIR-VLA通过行为层面的不变性与敏感性双重约束，有效提升VLA模型对多种分布外视觉偏移的鲁棒性，且学到的行为级RL指导具有跨视觉偏移类型的迁移能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) fine-tuning has shown promise for Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in robotic manipulation, but deployment-time visual shifts pose practical challenges. A key difficulty is that standard task rewards supervise task success, but offer limited guidance on whether a visual change is task-irrelevant or changes the behavior required for manipulation. We propose PAIR-VLA (Paired Action Invariance & Sensitivity for Visually Robust VLA), an RL fine-tuning framework to address this difficulty by adding two auxiliary objectives over paired visual variants during PPO optimization: an invariance term that reduces the discrepancy between action distributions for a task-preserving pair (e.g., different distractors), and a sensitivity objective that encourages separable action distributions for a task-altering pair (e.g., target object in a different pose). Together, these objectives turn visual variants from mere observation diversity into behavior-level guidance on policy responses during RL fine-tuning. We evaluate on ManiSkill3 across two representative VLA architectures, OpenVLA and $π_{0.5}$, under diverse out-of-distribution visual shifts including unseen distractors, texture changes, target object pose variation, viewpoint shifts, and lighting changes. Our method consistently improves over standard PPO, achieving average improvements of 16.62% on $π_{0.5}$ and 9.10% on OpenVLA. Notably, ablations further show generalization across visual shifts: invariance guidance learned from distractor and texture variants transfers to target-pose and lighting shifts, while adding sensitivity guidance on target-pose variants further improves robustness to nuisance shifts, highlighting the broader transferability of behavior-level RL guidance.

</details>


### [177] [SECOND-Grasp: Semantic Contact-guided Dexterous Grasping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13117)
*Han Yi Shin,Heeju Ko,Jaewon Mun,Qixing Huang,Jaehyeok Lee,Sung June Kim,Honglak Lee,Sujin Jang,Sangpil Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出SECOND-Grasp框架，通过视觉-语言推理生成接触提议，再经语义-几何一致性精炼得到3D接触图，最后通过逆运动学生成可行手部姿态用于策略学习，在灵巧抓取任务中同时实现物理稳定性和语义理解，抓取成功率在未见类别上达97.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灵巧抓取需要物理稳定交互与语义任务引导的协同，但现有工作常将两者视为分离目标，缺乏统一框架来同时实现物理稳定性与语义理解。

Method: 1) 通过视觉-语言推理生成粗略接触提议；2) 跨视图分割定位接触区域；3) 语义-几何一致性精炼（SGCR）跨视图语义一致性约束并去除几何无效区域，得到可靠3D接触图；4) 通过逆运动学为每个接触图推导可行手部姿态，生成策略学习监督信号。

Result: 在DexGraspNet上，已见类别抓取成功率98.2%，未见类别97.7%；意图感知抓取分别提升12.8%和26.2%；在Shadow Hand和Allegro Hand等额外数据集和机器人手上也表现良好。

Conclusion: SECOND-Grasp有效统一了物理稳定性与语义理解，在灵巧抓取任务中显著优于基线方法，具有较好的泛化能力。

Abstract: Achieving reliable robotic manipulation, such as dexterous grasping, requires a synergy between physically stable interactions and semantic task guidance, yet these objectives are often treated as separate, disjoint goals. In this paper, we investigate how to integrate dexterous grasping techniques, i.e., physically stable grasps for object lifting and language-guided grasp generation, to achieve both physical stability and semantic understanding. To this end, we propose SECOND-Grasp (SEmantic CONtact-guided Dexterous Grasping), a unified framework that enables robotic hands to dynamically adjust grasping strategies based on semantic reasoning while ensuring physical feasibility. We begin by obtaining coarse contact proposals through vision-language reasoning to infer where contacts should occur based on object properties, followed by segmentation to localize these regions across views. To further ensure consistency across multiple viewpoints, we introduce Semantic-Geometric Consistency Refinement (SGCR), which refines initial contact predictions by enforcing semantic consistency across views and removing geometrically invalid regions, yielding reliable 3D contact maps. Then, we derive a feasible hand pose for each contact map via inverse kinematics, generating a supervision signal for policy learning. Our approach, trained on DexGraspNet, consistently outperforms baselines in lifting success rate on both seen and unseen categories, achieving 98.2% and 97.7%, respectively, while also improving intent-aware grasping by 12.8% and 26.2%. We further show promising results on additional datasets and robotic hands, including Shadow Hand and Allegro Hand.

</details>


### [178] [Towards Long-horizon Embodied Agents with Tool-Aligned Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13119)
*Zixing Lei,Changxing Liu,Yichen Xiong,Minhao Xiong,Yuanzhuo Ding,Zhipeng Zhang,Weixin Li,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLAs-as-Tools 通过将长时域任务中的闭环规划负担分配给高层 VLM 智能体和多个专用 VLA 工具，并引入工具族接口和 Tool-Aligned Post-Training (TAPT) 方法，显著提升了机器人长时域操作的成功率和调用保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型在长时域任务中面临双重负担：既要进行长时间的闭环规划，又要执行多样的物理操作，导致性能受限。

Method: 提出 VLAs-as-Tools 策略：高层 VLM 智能体负责场景分析、全局规划和恢复；一组专用的 VLA 工具负责局部物理操作。引入 VLA 工具族接口，支持工具选择和执行进度反馈，实现事件触发的智能体重规划。提出 Tool-Aligned Post-Training (TAPT)，通过构建调用对齐的训练单元和工具族残差适配器，实现高效的工具专业化。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Long 上提升 π₀.₅ 成功率 4.8 个百分点，在 RoboTwin 上提升 23.1 个百分点；通过非偏比率（Non-biased Rate）衡量，调用保真度提升 15.0 个百分点。

Conclusion: VLAs-as-Tools 能有效解耦长时域任务中的规划与执行负担，提升任务成功率和调用可靠性，为 VLA 模型在复杂机器人任务中的应用提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models are effective robot action executors, but they remain limited on long-horizon tasks due to the dual burden of extended closed-loop planning and diverse physical operations. We therefore propose VLAs-as-Tools, a strategy that distributes this burden across a high-level vision language model (VLM) agent for temporal reasoning and a family of specialized VLA tools for diverse local physical operations. The VLM handles scene analysis, global planning, and recovery, while each VLA tool executes a bounded subtask. To tightly couple agent planning with VLA tool execution in long-horizon tasks, we introduce a VLA tool-family interface that exposes explicit tool selection and in-execution progress feedback, enabling efficient event-triggered agent replanning without continuous agent polling. To obtain diverse specialized VLA tools that faithfully follow agent invocations, we further propose Tool-Aligned Post-Training (TAPT), which constructs invocation-aligned training units for instruction following and adopts tool-family residual adapters for efficient tool specialization. Experiments show that VLAs-as-Tools improves the success rate of $π_{0.5}$ by 4.8 points on LIBERO-Long and 23.1 points on RoboTwin, and further enhances invocation fidelity by 15.0 points as measured by Non-biased Rate. Code will be released.

</details>


### [179] [Multi-Depth Uniform Coverage Path Planning for Unmanned Surface Vehicle Surveying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13123)
*Maider Larrazabal,Tong Yang,Izaro Goienetxea,Jaime Valls Miro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向无人水面艇测深测绘的自适应覆盖路径规划算法，通过利用先验深度信息动态调整测绘间距和波束孔径，解决了传统boustrophedon路径因忽略水深变化导致的海底覆盖不均匀问题，在合成和真实场景中均显著提升了覆盖率（合成地形>99%，真实港口>92%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统测深测绘路径规划（如boustrophedon方案）仅在海面均匀布设路径，未考虑多波束回声测深仪的探测范围受局部海底深度显著影响这一特性，导致海底覆盖不均匀。现有方法依赖手动选点且无法自适应调整，亟需一种能结合深度先验信息实现自动化、均匀海底覆盖的路径规划方案。

Method: 利用粗分辨率先验深度信息对目标区域进行预处理，自适应引导路径生成和传感范围配置；通过显式考虑深度变化，优化测绘测线间距并动态调整声学波束孔径，以在海底实现更一致的覆盖。

Result: 在挑战性合成地形中覆盖率达99%以上（传统boustrophedon路径最高仅75%）；在真实港口水深数据的仿真中覆盖率达92%以上（传统方法低于65%）。

Conclusion: 该算法相比传统方法在覆盖率上取得显著提升，且实现了完全自动化设计，适合自主无人水面艇实际应用，具有实用价值。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel automatic coverage path planning algorithm for bathymetry surveying with unmanned surface vehicles. The detection range of the mapping sensor employed - a multibeam echo sounder - is heavily influenced by local seafloor depths. Hence, a path designed to uniformly cover the sea surface does not guarantee uniform coverage of the seafloor. Yet this is currently the typical process for bathymetric surveys, with the simplistic boustrophedon scheme along manually selected waypoints at constant depths being the most widespread planner used. The proposed scheme incorporates coarse prior depth information to pre-process the target region and adaptively guide path generation and sensing range configuration. By explicitly accounting for depth variations, the proposed algorithm designs a coverage path with optimised spacing between survey passes that adjusts the sensing beam aperture to achieve more consistent seafloor coverage. The proposed method is shown to offer significant improvements in both synthetic and real-world scenarios. Validations in challenging synthetic terrains achieves coverage ratios beyond 99%, a marked improvement when compared with traditional boustrophedon paths revealing a maximum 75% coverage. The same trend appears in realistic simulations using real bathymetric data from a coastal harbour, with coverage reaching over 92%, and significantly surpassing boustrophedon sweeps with coverage rates below 65%.
